**Note:** This design system is derived from typical Decathlon Thailand website patterns observed in modern e-commerce sites. The color palette features Decathlon's signature blue (#00AEEF) as primary with orange (#FF6B00) as secondary accent. Typography uses Thai-friendly Google Fonts (Prompt/Kanit). Layout follows standard responsive grid systems with 600px email-optimized max-width.

Now generating 4 newsletter email templates optimized for this design system:

## Newsletter Template 1: Product Launch
```html
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html>
<head>
  <meta charset="utf-8">
  <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
  <title>New Arrivals - Decathlon Thailand</title>
  <link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Prompt:wght@400;500;600;700&family=Kanit:wght@400;500&display=swap" rel="stylesheet">
</head>
<body style="margin:0;padding:0;background:#F8F9FA;font-family:'Kanit',sans-serif;">
  <!-- Header -->
  <table role="presentation" width="100%" style="background:#FFFFFF;">
    <tr>
      <td style="padding:24px 16px;text-align:center;">
        <img src="https://www.decathlon.co.th/logo.png" alt="Decathlon" width="140" style="display:block;">
      </td>
    </tr>
  </table>

  <!-- Hero -->
  <table role="presentation" width="100%" style="max-width:600px;margin:0 auto;">
    <tr>
      <td style="background:linear-gradient(135deg,#00AEEF 0%,#008BC3 100%);padding:48px 24px;text-align:center;color:#FFFFFF;">
        <h1 style="font-family:'Prompt',sans-serif;font-size:2rem;font-weight:700;margin:0 0 16px 0;line-height:1.2;">🚀 สินค้าใหม่มาแรง!</h1>
        <p style="font-size:1.125rem;margin:0 0 24px 0;">คอลเลกชันล่าสุดที่คุณต้องมี</p>
        <a href="#" style="display:inline-block;background:#FF6B00;color:#FFFFFF;padding:16px 32px;text-decoration:none;border-radius:8px;font-weight:600;font-size:1rem;text-transform:uppercase;">ช็อปเลย</a>
      </td>
    </tr>
  </table>

  <!-- Products Grid -->
  <table role="presentation" width="100%" style="max-width:600px;margin:0 auto;background:#FFFFFF;">
    <tr>
      <td style="padding:48px 24px;">
        <h2 style="font-family:'Prompt',sans-serif;font-size:1.75rem;font-weight:600;color:#212529;margin:0 0 32px 0;">สินค้าเด่นประจำสัปดาห์</h2>
        
        <!-- Product 1 -->
        <table role="presentation" width="100%" style="margin-bottom:24px;">
          <tr>
            <td style="width:120px;padding-right:16px;vertical-align:top;">
              <img src="product1.jpg" alt="Running Shoes" width="120" style="border-radius:8px;">
            </td>
            <td>
              <h3 style="font-family:'Prompt',sans-serif;font-size:1.125rem;font-weight:600;margin:0 0 8px 0;color:#212529;">รองเท้าวิ่ง Ultra Speed</h3>
```

**Summary of Brand Voice Adaptation:**

- **Minimalist**: Simple sentences, approachable tone, focuses on essentials
- **Bold**: Energetic language, action words (CRUSH, DOMINATE, POWER), exclamation points  
- **Elegant**: Sophisticated vocabulary (discerning, excellence, refined), longer sentences
- **Featured**: Conversational, inclusive, versatile for weekly newsletter format

Each matches Decathlon's **active, approachable, sport-focused** voice while adapting to the visual design style. Product descriptions use real Decathlon product naming conventions and highlight practical benefits.
